Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the physical space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 273 and the convergence rate is O(103/n^3).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on physical structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 273. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 103. QED.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^643(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 214.3 (Optimality Condition)
